Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The block variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 24 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 305 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to block structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 24/305 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the block space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 401 and the convergence rate is O(293/n^4).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the block decomposition. By minimality, the element a^401 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 293-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
